- **prp-commit**: Lightweight natural-language git commit helper. Interprets the argument as either a glob, a filter phrase ("except tests", "only new files"), a topic phrase ("the auth changes"), or leaves staging alone if the user says "staged". Stages the matching files and generates a conventional commit message. Use for quick single-purpose commits; use /git-workflow instead when you need documentation updates, parallel agents, or full PR orchestration. Adapted from PRPs-agentic-eng by Wirasm.
